       i.      To complete the bid process, each nurse will be assigned a five (5) minute
               block of time to submit the bid. Staff are responsible for submitting their
               bids in person, telephone or email to the manager conducting the bid and
               the designated AFSCME Representative.           Employees who fail to
               successfully bid or who are ineligible to bid will be assigned at the
               discretion of the Medical Services Manager or designee.

       j.      Bids must be submitted in writing to the manager or designee in person or
               by phone at the time of the bid. All phone bids will be validated by a Union
               represented member and signed by the manager or designee.

       k.      The Union will provide a Union representative to attend the bid process.

       l.      Should Institution management decide to redeploy staff to a different
               shift/days off, other than flex staff, they will first seek qualified volunteers
               to fill the vacancy or long term absence. If there is more than one (1)
               volunteer, the position shall be offered to the volunteer with the highest
               seniority. If no employee volunteers, Institution management will move
               the employee(s) with the lowest seniority to the necessary shift.

       m.      Once a bid has occurred, and when a position becomes vacant that the
               Institution intends to fill, and less than one half (1/2) of the bid cycle is
               completed, the Institution will open for bid shift/days off to all employees of
               the vacant position. Employees will notify the Institution within seven (7)
               calendar days of their interest in the shift/days off schedule. The
               Institution will assign the most senior employee the schedule. The
               resulting vacancy will be filled by Institution management. The selected



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 66                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               employee will start the new schedule on the first following Sunday without
               any pay penalty.

       n.      Non Bid Assignments. The Infection Control/Chronic Disease Registered
               Nurse shall be a non bid assignment. Interested individuals may apply
               and interview for the assignment and Medical manager or designee will
               select an employee for the assignment.

3.     Vacation Bidding.
       1.    For purposes of vacation bidding, seniority is defined as total length of
             State Service. Employees will bid through a rotation based on seniority,
             through four (4) complete cycles of all employees eligible to bid for
             vacation. Each represented staff member will be able to bid for one (1)
             continuous block of time on each bid cycle or until such time that the
             vacation calendar is full. Staff will be responsible to contact management
             at the time of the bid.
             a.      The Union will provide a Union representative to attend the bid
                     process.

               b.      There will be a total of three (3) represented staff members allowed
                       off for vacation purposes per week during the months of June, July,
                       August, the last two (2) weeks of March and the weeks during
                       Thanksgiving and Christmas. This is to accommodate all the staff
                       members’ adequate time to ensure usage of their vacation time.

               c.      During the months of June, July, August and December each
                       employee will be limited to a maximum of two (2) weeks of vacation
                       per each bid rotation.

               d.        Returned Vacation
                         If any annual bid time off is returned by bargaining unit employees,
                         or future bid time off for employees that leave SRCI Health
                         Services, the bargaining unit employees will have the opportunity to
                         use those vacation weeks via the following:
                      I.        The Nurse Manager or designee within seven (7) calendar
                                days will send out an email to all Nurses announcing the bid
                                vacation day(s) returned. Employees shall have five (5)
                                calendar days to send a reply email indicating they would
                                like to use that time off.

                     II.      At the end of five (5) calendar days, all email replies will be
                              placed into a hat and a lottery draw will occur. The lottery
                              draw will be conducted in the presence of the Nurse


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 67                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
                              Manager (or designee) a Lead Worker and an uninvolved
                              Union Nurse member. If no reply emails are received within
                              the five (5) calendar day period, the vacation time off can be
                              used by the Institution as it chooses. Bid time off returned
                              within thirty (30) calendar days or less of the scheduled time
                              off shall be used by the Institution as it chooses.

                       e.     When an employee requests time off for vacation (outside
                              the normal bidding process), requests must be submitted to
                              management by the fifteenth (15th) of the month prior to the
                              month of the requested days off. The employee will be
                              notified in writing of acceptance or denial by the 20th of the
                              month prior to the month of the requested days off.

                       f.     Requests for time off outside of the vacation bid process that
                              are submitted after the fifteenth (15th) of the month will be
                              approved or denied within five (5) days of the receipt of the
                              request.

       2.      For purposes of “job sharing positions” the two (2) employees whom share
               the one (1) full-time position, shall be able to split the total monthly time
               within the bid shift among themselves in any format.

4.     Overtime
       a.    Voluntary Unplanned Overtime.
             i.    Overtime shall be voluntary except during periods of emergency, or
                   unless Management is unable to fill a work assignment by voluntary
                   means.

               ii.     Overtime shall be first offered to staff working on shift.

               iii.    Staff will sign up for voluntary overtime by indicating what days and
                       shifts they are willing to work.

               iv.     If circumstances require a short extension of a shift to complete a
                       task, the Institution may involuntarily require the involved staff
                       member to stay and complete the assignment. Such extensions
                       shall not affect the staff’s placement on the mandatory overtime
                       lists unless the staff member is required to work more than one (1)
                       hour.
               v.      If staff works voluntary overtime it will count toward fulfilling staff’s
                       mandatory overtime obligation.



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 68                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
       b.      Voluntary Planned Overtime.
               i.    Known voluntary overtime opportunities shall be posted by Friday
                     of the preceding week.

               ii.     If more than one (1) staff member volunteers for voluntary overtime,
                       the first priority will be granted to the first staff member that signs
                       up from the required shift (on their days off). Secondary priority
                       shall be granted to the employee who volunteers to work the largest
                       number of hours to cover the anticipated need.

               iii.    If staff works voluntary overtime it will count toward fulfilling staff’s
                       mandatory overtime obligation.

       c.      Mandatory Overtime.
               i.   Overtime shall be voluntary except during emergencies, or unless
                    management is unable to fill a work assignment by voluntary
                    means.

               ii.     If there are no volunteers to work overtime, the Institution will
                       assign overtime using the mandatory (bucket) overtime list.

               iii.    The mandatory overtime (bucket) list shall be updated. The list
                       shall be posted in the nurse’s chart room and shall be emailed to
                       the employees as changed occur.

               iv.     Staff may not be selected for mandatory overtime on those days
                       which are considered the staff member’s Friday. This does not
                       include false Fridays.

               v.      Staff will be placed on the mandatory overtime list by alphabetical
                       order using the staff member last name on each shift.

               vi.     When mandatory overtime must be assigned, the Institution shall
                       assign the work to the next staff member available on the list who
                       has not worked mandatory overtime.

               vii.    Staff will provide current contact numbers to the Medical Services
                       Manager or designee.

5.     Time Trades
       All Time Trades within SRCI Health Services will be completed within a sixty (60)
       calendar day period or the end of the bid cycle whichever is shorter. Unlimited
       shift and days off trades will be authorized, as long as they are in compliance


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 69                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
       with Article 25, section 7 of the Collective Bargaining Agreement. Trading
       assigned duties must have prior management approval. The completion period
       may be extended at the request of the affected employee with the consent of the
       Medical Services Managers or designee.

6.     Bargaining unit staff will be permitted up to a one (1) two-month block of
       continuous time trade each bid cycle under its Article 25 language, subject to
       denial for valid cause. Valid cause could include too many staff on block time
       trades at the same time. Anything over the eight-week block of time traded
       would be considered circumventing the bidding process language under the
       CBA, whether in the same time trade request or a subsequent block trade
       request after exhausting the eight-week allotment.

7.     This includes requests for time trades where the shifts are of uneven length,
       which is subject to the CBA language regarding additional costs to the
       Department for time trades, and all other applicable CBA articles.

8.     Granting of the eight-week block does not affect staff’s ability to request time
       trades for days off during the bidding cycle. Staff can still request time trades for
       days and the CBA language regarding ‘unlimited time trades shall be granted
       unless there is a valid cause’ will still be applicable for those requests.

9.     Nothing in this specific Article changes the authority or ability of the Agency to
       change an employee’s work schedule to meet Agency operating needs in
       accordance with the Agreement.

TRCI:
1.    Health Services.
      1.    All bargaining unit Registered Nurses and Health Services Techs are
            encouraged to develop a schedule and submit that schedule to
            management.

       2.      Management will review all schedules submitted by staff to assure that the
               needs of the institution are met.

       3.      All Agency approved schedules are then displayed no less than thirty (30)
               days before the bid.

       4.      Each nurse will then vote on the six (6) month staff schedule they prefer.

       5.      The six (6) month staffing schedule that is preferred by the majority of
               nurses will be used for bidding the next six (6) month work schedule.



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               70                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
       6.      The bid is done in order of seniority. For the purposes of this Agreement
               seniority will be defined as Agency time in classification as a Registered
               Nurse or Health Service Technicians/Licensed Practical Nurse (RN or
               HST/LPN). Each RN or HST/LPN will have ten (10) minutes to bid. Staff
               will be able to place their bid in person, by telephone, or via e-mail, prior to
               the bid after the schedule is posted. (More than one (1) bid may be
               placed via e-mail in order of preference, with the understanding that if the
               schedule bid requests are not available the employee will move to the
               bottom of the list). Any RN or HST/LPN missing their time slot, will be
               moved to the bottom of the list and will bid last. A Union Representative
               and a Manager will be present during the schedule bidding. Trial service
               employees shall not be eligible to bid until they successfully complete their
               trial service period.

       7.      Rotations will start the first Sunday in January and the first Sunday in July
               of each year.


       8.      Non Bid Assignments:
               (a)   Infection Control/Chronic Disease and Provider Clinic shall be non
                     bid assignments. Non bid assignments will be reviewed annually.

               (b)     These assignments will be filled by interested and appropriately
                       credentialed interested individuals. Management will interview
                       interested and appropriately credentialed individuals and thereafter
                       select employees for the assignments.

2.     TRCI Vacation Bidding – Registered Nurse and Health Services Technicians.
       a.    Vacations will be bid on the basis of seniority which is based upon the
             definition in Article 32, Section 8, of the Parties Collective Bargaining
             Agreement.

       b.      The annual vacation bid will occur during the first (1st) full week of
               November. A Union representative and a Management representative
               shall be present each day.

       c.      Management will post a list of employees by seniority including their
               seniority date a minimum of seven (7) days before the bid. The list will
               also include the assigned time for employees to place their bids in ten (10)
               minute increments during which the employee will place their vacation
               request. Any employees on leave or absent at the time of posting this list,
               and not expected to return prior to the vacation bid, will be contacted and



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 71                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               provided this information in order to ensure they may submit their bid
               should they wish.

       d.      If an employee fails to place their bid in person, or by phone, during their
               allotted time, they may bid at the next available time but they shall not be
               able to bump another employee who has already bid during their allotted
               time.

       e.      Employee will bid vacation utilizing the following matrix:

               Years of State Service at Time of Bid     Number of Weeks
               1.    15 Years or more                    6 weeks
               2.    From 10 up to 15 Years              5 weeks
               3.    From 5 up to 10 Years               4 weeks
               4.    From 3 up to 5 Years                3 weeks
               5.    From 0 up to 3 Years                2 weeks.

       f.      There will be one (1) bid process where employees may bid a consecutive
               block of time or may spread the designated number of weeks over
               different months with the following conditions:
               1.     Employees may only bid a maximum of two (2) weeks during the
                      months of June, July or August.

               2.      Employees may only bid a maximum of one (1) vacation week
                       during the weeks that encompasses the month of March, or the
                       week of Thanksgiving or the week of Christmas selecting only one
                       (1) of those choices.

               3.      Following the completion of the first bid, a second will take place in
                       order of seniority only if open slots remain. During the second,
                       employees may bid for as many one (1) week slots as are available
                       to bid regardless of the above matrix and if they still have or will
                       accrue sufficient vacation time to use at the time it is taken.

       l.      Regardless of the above matrix, employees may only be able to bid based
               upon the hours accrued and what they will earn prior to the time the
               vacation actually occurs but at no time will they be allowed to bid more
               than the number of weeks that correspond to their length of service that
               are listed in the above matrix.

       J.      Prior to the vacation bid occurring, a report of the accrued leave banks will
               be produced. There will be a minimum of three (3) employees off during
               the months of March, July and August and the week of Thanksgiving and


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                72                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               the week of Christmas. For March, July and August the third (3rd) vacation
               time off will begin the first (1st) full week and continue for thirty (30)
               calendar days. The remainder of the year will have a minimum of two (2)
               employees off for their bid vacation.

       K.      Vacation requests may be made in person or by telephone with the union
               representative present. E-mail requests will also be accepted. If the
               employee fails to make their vacation request, by any of the above means,
               within their allotted time frame, they will then go to the bottom of the list for
               that day of bidding.

       L.      Vacation bids are based on earned accrued vacation hours only,
               excluding any other paid time off. Bids may include projected vacation
               hours to be earned by the time of actual vacation time off.

       M.     Float Pool Nurses cannot bid vacation. The Infection Control/Chronic
              Disease position will not affect or be part of the vacation bid process, as
              that position is not covered during their vacation periods.

3.     TRCI Medical Services Overtime Process
       Definitions:
       1.      Long range planned overtime: a shift of four (4) hours or more of needed
               coverage that is known more than five (5) days in advance.

       2.      Short range unplanned overtime: a shift of four (4) hours or more of
               needed coverage that is known in five (5) days or less.

       3.      Mandated Overtime: overtime that is needed that day or the following shift
               and nobody has volunteered to work the hours under the short range
               unplanned overtime process.

       4.    False Friday: the new last day of an employees work week when his or
             her normal last day is traded with another employee.
       Exceptions:
       1.    HST’s cannot be assigned without an RN on shift.

       2.      Any voluntary overtime of less than four (4) hours will not constitute an
               overtime point and will not follow the overtime process.

       3.      Staff that are still orienting will not be mandated.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 73                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
       Long-Range Planned Overtime Process:
       1.    Staff that wishes to be contacted for long range planned overtime will sign
             up on the overtime calendar for the specific days they are available to
             work by the 20th of the previous month. (Must sign each day of the month
             that they are available).

       2.      Staff will receive one (1) point for each shift of four (4) hours or more that
               they are scheduled and actually worked.

       3.      The overtime point sheet runs for six (6) months (January 1 – June 30 and
               July 1 – December 30).

       4.      The first overtime assignment, following the implementation of the bidded
               schedule, of four (4) hours or more, is scheduled by seniority and after
               that, it is by the person with the least amount of points. If two (2) people
               have the same amount of points, then it would be scheduled by seniority.

       5.      When long range overtime is available, management, or their designee,
               will use the overtime calendar and will make one (1) attempt to contact the
               people that are signed up for that date. The person with the least amount
               of points will be given the shift (or the most seniority if there are no points).

       6.      If someone volunteers for a long range overtime and does not complete
               their obligation, they will be given an additional two (2) points (total of
               three (3)), unless this is due to an approved FMLA/OFLA or SAIF
               qualifying condition.

       7.      Any overtime worked during a week, where the staff working that overtime
               calls out sick for another shift during the same week, and the call out is not
               FMLA/OFLA protected, shall result in the staff receiving two (2) additional
               points.

       8.      If no one volunteers for the shift, the short term unplanned overtime
               process will be implemented when it is five (5) days or less away.

       9.      Employees cannot trade overtime shifts. If employees are unable to fulfill
               overtime obligations, management must be notified.

       Short-Range, Unplanned Overtime Process:
       1.    If there is a shift of four (4) hours or more that needs to be filled in five (5)
             days or less, management, or designee, will use the overtime schedule
             and make one (1) attempt to contact staff who are signed up for that date.



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 74                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
               Staff accepting and working the overtime assignment will receive one (1)
               point for each shift of four (4) hours or more.

       2.      Any overtime worked during a week, where the staff working that overtime
               calls out sick for another shift during the same week, and the call out is not
               FMLA/OFLA protected, shall result in the staff receiving two (2) additional
               points.

       3.      A phone call will be made to the number on the Health Services Recall list
               if the person is not on site. If the person is on site, management, or
               designee, will talk to them in person.


       4.      If a staff member receives a phone message asking them to work an
               overtime shift, they must call back and verify, prior to coming in, that the
               shift is still open for overtime.

       5.      If the overtime shift has not been filled, management, or designee, will ask
               for volunteers among staff on duty. If no one volunteers, as a last resort,
               the overtime will be mandated.

       6.      Mandated Overtime Process:
               1.   Employees on duty, including those on trades, will be mandated on
                    a rotation basis.

               2.      Only employees that are on duty can be mandated and
                       management will make every effort to not mandate an employee on
                       their “Friday” (this does not include “false Fridays”).

               3.      When an employee has volunteered for an overtime shift of two (2)
                       hours or more, the employee will be moved to the bottom of the
                       mandatory overtime list.

WCCF: Health Services – RNs
Section 1. Definition of Seniority.
Seniority for shifts and days off is defined as time in classification within the Agency.

Section 2. Overtime.
a.    Overtime shall be voluntary except during periods of emergency, or unless
      Management is unable to fill a work assignment by voluntary means.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                75                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
b.     Unplanned Overtime.
       1.    When management determines overtime needs to be worked,
             management will first ask the employees on shift if they are interested in
             working the overtime assignment. If that employee does not accept the
             overtime opportunity, management will contact other employees to
             volunteer to work the overtime.

       2.      If there are no volunteers to work overtime, management will assign
               mandatory overtime.

       3.      Mandatory overtime will be assigned on a rotation basis using the seniority
               list initially by inverse order. Movement to the bottom of the list requires
               an employee to work a minimum of four (4) hours during an overtime shift.
               Where the Institution requires an employee to work on a scheduled day
               off, work will be assigned in a block of time of no less than four (4) hours.

c.     Planned Overtime.
            1.     Where management anticipates there will be a need to work
                   overtime in the future, management will solicit volunteers by use of
                   a signup sheet posted on the bulletin board.

              2.       If there are no volunteers, management will assign mandatory
                       overtime pursuant to section 2(b)(3) of this Article.

Section 3. Work Scheduling.
a.    The Parties agree to a varying work schedule for employees that has been
      agreed upon by employees and management. Notwithstanding Article 25,
      Section 9, overtime, for purposes of this Section, will be paid for time worked
      beyond posted/scheduled shift hours.

b.     A variable work schedule is defined as follows: “A variable work schedule is a
       schedule which may vary the number of hours worked on a daily basis within a
       workweek (combination of eight (8) ten (10) and twelve (12) hour shifts) and the
       sequence of these hours, but does not exceed forty (40) hours in a workweek as
       defined in Article 25, Section 1. The variable work schedule will be posted in
       advance which is agreed upon in advance by the employee and supervisor.

c.     Nothing in this Agreement changes the authority of the Agency to change an
       employee’s work schedule to meet the Agency’s operational needs in
       accordance with applicable provision of the collective Bargaining Agreement. In
       the absence of mutual agreement for a varied schedule, management may




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               76                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
d.     create a schedule based upon operatorial needs, which will be filled using time in
       classification seniority

       This Section does not establish any additional obligation on the Employer,
       Agency or the Union to negotiate different workweek or overtime work definitions
       for the bargaining unit employees..

Section 4. Vacation Bidding.
a.    Vacation bidding seniority is defined as state service in accordance with Article
      32. Annual vacation bidding shall take place two (2) weeks after the shift/days
      off bid takes place in September of each year.

b.     Management will establish the vacation weeks. Known planned (staff) absences
       and training weeks will not be available for the annual vacation bid.

c.     Vacation time must be taken in one (1) week increments. During the first (1st),
       second (2nd) and third (3rd) rounds of bidding, staff can bid up to two (2) weeks of
       vacation time off. These weeks can be non-consecutive.

d.     Staff can only bid for the amount of accrued vacation time they will have at the
       vacation period requested.

e.     To complete bidding, each RN will be designated a ten (10) minute block of time
       in order of seniority. Bids can be submitted in person, telephone or email. A
       designated Union representative and manager will be present during the bidding.

f.     If bidded vacation time is returned it can be made available at the manager’s
       discretion on a first (1st) come, first (1st) serve basis.

g.     If the RN does not have enough accrued vacation hours, the vacation will be
       cancelled or reduced to match the RN’s accrued hours after discussion with the
       RN.

h.     Vacation request outside the normal bidding process need to be submitted by the
       tenth (10th) of the month prior to the requested time off. Approval or denial of the
       submitted request will be made no later than the twentieth (20th) of the month
       prior to the requested days. Single vacation day off requests within the month
       will be reviewed subject to Institution operating needs.

i.     Vacation weeks cannot be traded.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus              77                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
C.   Central Distribution: Truck Drivers II
The Parties agree to the following for the Truck Drivers II at Central Distribution Center:

Seniority for shift and days off and/or routes will be determined based on time within the
classification of Truck Driver II. In the case of a tie, time within the AFSCME Security
Plus Bargaining Unit will be used as the tie breaker.
1.      Job Bidding: Bidding will occur every six (6) months effective the first Sunday in
        July and January. The Truck Driver II’s will bid their scheduled days off by
        seniority starting with the most senior employee. All available shifts and days off
        are to be posted by Management two (2) weeks prior to bid date.
        a.     The current route designated as route number 3, that is in effect as of
               January 1, 2021, is considered exempt from the above bid process.

       b.      Route number 3 is exempt for the purpose of being reserved for the
               employee who is assigned as a Truck Driver Leadworker.

       c.      Should the Agency determine a need to revise route number 3 or no
               longer assign a Leadworker, then subsection a-c of this Section shall be
               discussed with AFSCME.

2.     Overtime: Management shall post overtime needs outside of the regular routes
       which are known in advance. Employees shall indicate their willingness to work
       it and the overtime will be assigned according to seniority as defined above
       provided that the anticipated assignment of the run will not exceed the maximum
       drive hours. Unforeseen and/or emergent needs for overtime can be assigned
       based upon the availability of the most senior Truck Drivers II who volunteers.

       For purposes of overtime assignments, the term ‘availability’ in subsection 2 at
       paragraph 1 above, shall be defined as: 1) the Truck Driver II has seniority to be
       selected for the overtime; 2) the Truck Driver II is available for their next shift; 3)
       the Truck Driver II has not exceeded their maximum drive hours. Overtime
       assignments do not replace Truck Driver II’s regular bidded shift.

D.     CCCF: Food Services
1.     Coffee Creek Correctional Facility Food Services will bid for shift and days off
       based on DOC time in classification Seniority for all positions within Culinary
       including the Bakery and Warehouse positions. The Bakery and Warehouse
       positions will be bid every two (2) years in alternating years. Effective July 01,
       2011, employees who transfer, promote or demote into CCCF Food Service
       Coordinator positions the definition of seniority for job bidding shall be AFSCME
       Security Plus Time In Classification. Employees within CCCF Food Services
       before that date shall maintain the seniority dates and definition as of June 30,
       2011.


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                78                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
       The bid process for all other assignments will take place every six (6) months
       with bids taking place in the months of June and December. Staff will be notified
       of the results of their bid in the months of July and January with the new bid
       schedules beginning in August and February. If two (2) or more employees have
       the same DOC seniority date, the order of seniority will be set by a one-time –
       double blind lottery process. The only way the above process would change is a
       written agreement between the Union and management.

2.     Overtime: Overtime shall be voluntary except during periods of emergency or
       when Management is unable to fill a work assignment through voluntary means.
       a.    Voluntary Overtime.
             Before the monthly calendar schedule is posted, overtime will be offered
             on a first come first serve basis. Once the monthly schedule is posted,
             voluntary overtime will be assigned using the voluntary sign up list. A
             monthly calendar will be posted for staff to sign up for voluntary overtime.
             The calendar will list each shift and assignment for the entire month. Each
             list will have a column for employees name and scheduled shift/days off.
             The seniority date shall be attached to the list. If there are two (2) or more
             employees asking for overtime on the same day, the most senior
             employee will be asked first when an overtime opportunity becomes
             available for that date. If no employee has signed up, or those on the date
             turn down the time, other staff will be asked to fill in on a voluntary basis
             from the overtime list based on most senior employee to least senior.

               If a staff signs up for overtime then chooses to turn down the time on two
               (2) different occasions, their name will be removed from the calendar and
               not allowed to sign up again for thirty (30) days from the date of the
               second decline.

       b.      Mandatory Overtime.
               If there are no volunteers for overtime or in an emergency, staff can be
               assigned from the mandatory (bucket) overtime list to fill the shift. Before
               assigning mandatory overtime, a good faith effort must be made and
               documented by management, to fill overtime assignment through
               voluntary means.

               A mandatory overtime list shall be established based on inverse seniority.
               When mandatory overtime is required to be worked for the next shift, it will
               be assigned to employees on duty in inverse order of seniority. Once an
               employee works a mandatory overtime assignment, the employee’s name
               will be moved to the bottom of the list. An employee will only be subject to
               one (1) mandatory overtime per employee work week unless all other staff
               on duty have been assigned mandatory overtime. Employees shall not


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               79                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               normally be required to work mandatory overtime at the conclusion of their
               scheduled ‘Friday’.

               If circumstances require a short extension of the shift to complete an
               assignment, management may assign the affected employee to stay and
               complete the assignment. Any assignment that extends beyond an
               employee’s regular shift will cause their name to be rotated to the bottom
               of the list provided the employee works two (2) hours or more.

               Mandatory overtime assignments can be split. It will be the employee’s
               responsibility to find another employee to split the shift with and the
               bucketed employee is responsible for coverage of the complete shift. The
               bucketed employee shall notify their supervisor of the split overtime shift
               and the other employee’s name and the hours to be worked by both
               employees. A split shift can be split with no more than two (2) employees.
               If both employees work more than two (2) hours, they will be rotated to the
               bottom of the mandatory overtime list. In this case, the split overtime
               employee will be rotated first (1st) followed by the bucketed employee who
               will be rotated to the bottom of the mandatory overtime list.

E.     CCCF: Physical Plant
       Upon request, work schedules in the Physical Plant may be straight eights (8s) or
       straight tens (10s) in circumstances where employees continue to supervise
       Adults in Custody or otherwise subject to prior management approval.

       Overtime Assignment
       a.    If circumstances require an extension of an employee’s shift for up to four
             (4) hours to complete a task already assigned, management may require
             the employee to complete the assignment.

       b.      If management determines that overtime work is necessary and does not
               require special licensure, management will contact qualified staff who
               have signed up on the volunteer overtime list, and will be filled in order of
               seniority. In the rare event that management determines a particular
               skillset is needed for a specific job, management may select the Physical
               Plant staff with that skillset for that overtime opportunity. Once the
               employee has worked the assigned overtime, their name will rotate to the
               bottom of the list. If an employee on the list is offered the overtime and
               refuses, they will be moved to the bottom of the list. Once the list has
               been created employes may voluntarily add or remove their name at any
               time. If a name is added, it will be added to the bottom of the list. If there
               are no volunteers, management shall use          professional discretion in




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                80                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               offering overtime to other qualified employees that can perform the duties
               in order of seniority.

       c.      Employees may provide management with up to and no more than two (2)
               accurate contact phone numbers, in writing. Employee will update their
               numbers to management as needed. Each phone number listed for the
               employee will be attempted before continuing down the list. A message
               may be left, but management will continue calling employees who are
               qualified to perform the work for the overtime until someone accepts the
               assignment.

       d.      For purposes of this Section, seniority shall be defined as time in class
               within the Agency.

       e.      As a last resort, when employer provided uniforms are not issued in a
               timely manner, Physical Plant employees will be authorized to use regular
               paid time to purchase necessary uniforms which will be reimbursed.

F.    CRCI: Food Service
Section 1. Shift and Days Off Bid.
1.    Columbia River Correctional Institution Food Services will bid for Shift and Days
      Off based on a seniority definition of time in classification in the AFSCME
      Security Plus bargaining unit for all positions with Culinary.

2.     The bid process for all assignments will take place every six (6) months with bids
       taking place in the months of May and November. Staff will be notified of the
       result of their bid in the months of June and December within the new bid
       schedules taking effect the first Sunday after January 1st and the first Sunday
       after July 4th. If two (2) or more employees have the same DOC seniority date,
       the order of seniority will be set by a one-time double blind lottery process.

Section 2. Overtime.
1.    Voluntary Overtime.
      a.    Planned Overtime: (a) When management determines there is a need for
            over that management knows must be worked the following day and/or
            beyond, management will use the voluntary overtime list and offer the
            overtime opportunity to Food Service Coordinators, on a rotating basis,
            based on seniority. Seniority shall be defined as Agency time in
            classification. Management will first offer the overtime to the most senior
            Food Service Coordinator on the list.

       b.      Management will move through the list until a Food Service coordinator
               volunteers for the available overtime.

       c.      The employee will move to the bottom of the list if they:
               1.    Work the overtime offered;



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                81                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               2.      The Food Service Coordinator is offered the overtime opportunity
                       but turns it down; or

               3.      Management calls the Food Service Coordinator at home, but the
                       Food Service Coordinator does not respond. The Agency will only
                       be required to call the Food Service Coordinator one (1) time
                       before moving to the next Food Service Coordinator on the list
                       (Food Services Coordinators will provide the Agency a current
                       telephone number and keep it updated as needed).

2.     Unplanned Overtime.
       a.    When management determines there is a need for overtime to be worked
             on the day of the shift, management will first offer the overtime opportunity
             to the most senior employee on shift.

       b.      If the most senior Food Service Coordinator turns down the overtime
               opportunity, then management will ask the next senior employee on shift.

3.     Mandatory Overtime.
       a.   If there are no volunteers to work overtime, the overtime will be assigned
            using the mandatory overtime (bucket) list.

       b.      A mandatory overtime list, in revers order of seniority, of all Food Service
               Coordinators shall be maintained in the food service office and will be
               accessible to all employees in the Food Service Coordinator folder on the
               (P) drive.

Section 3. Vacation Bidding.
Annual vacation bidding will be done during the third (3rd) week of November for the
following calendar year. A representative from the Union will be present during the
bidding process.
       a.     Employees can bid up to four (4) continuous blocks of time in each of the
              two (2) rounds of vacation bidding.

       b.      Management will award available vacation based upon seniority. Once
               the most senior employee has had their first block of vacation approved,
               the bid moves to the next most senior employee, and if available, will have
               their first block of vacation choice approved. The process continues until
               each employee’s bid has had their first vacation continuous block
               approved. Within the same bid, management will then move down the list
               again and approve available vacation time based upon seniority, until
               seniority has been used to award each of the four (4) continuous blocks
               identified by the employee’s bid.

       c.      Employees can only bid for the amount of accrued vacation time they will
               have for the time request. If the employee does not have enough hours
               accrued for the hours of vacation requested, the vacation will be cancelled
               or reduced to match the employee’s accrued hours following discussion
               with the employee.


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               82                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
       d.      For purposes of vacation bidding, seniority is defined as total length of
               State Service. Employees will bid through a rotation based on seniority,
               through four (4) complete cycles of all employees eligible to bid for
               vacation. Each represented staff member will be able to bid for one (1)
               continuous block of time on each bid cycle or until such time that the
               vacation calendar is full. Staff will be responsible to contact management
               at the time of the bid.

Physical Plant
1.    Assignment of On-Call Coverage
      a.     Effective on November 10, 2014 a list of all Physical Plant staff and their
             state seniority dates will be provided to the Physical Plant Manager by the
             AFSCME Security Plus Union President for CRCI.

       b.      Management will designate on-call coverage on a rotation basis to all
               employees based on the Union seniority list (State seniority). Employees
               may provide the Manager with no more than two (2) accurate contact
               phone numbers, in writing. Each phone number listed for the employee
               will be attempted before continuing down the list.

       c.      A message may be left, but the Manger will continue calling employees for
               the on-call coverage until someone has accepted the assignment.

       d.      Once an employee has accepted and filled the assignment or if the
               employee declines, their name will go to the bottom of the list.

       e.      New CRCI FMS employees will be added to the bottom of the list.

       f.      If there are no volunteers for an on-call assignment, the Institution will
               assign the employee who is at the top of the on-call list to work the on-call
               assignment. If an employee is mandated to work on-call, they will move to
               the bottom of the on-call list.

2.     Overtime
       a.    Effective on November 10, 2014 a list of all Physical Plant staff and their
             State seniority dates will be provided to the Physical Plant Manager by the
             AFSCME Security Plus Union President for CRCI.

       b.      Management will designate overtime on a rotation basis to all employee
               based on the Union seniority list (State seniority). Employees may provide
               the Manager with no more than two (2) accurate contact phone numbers,
               in writing. Each phone number listed for the employee will be attempted
               before continuing down the list. A message may be left, but the Manager



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               83                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               will continue calling employees for the overtime until someone has
               accepted the assignment.

       c.      Once an employee has accepted and fill the assignment or if the
               employee declines, their name will go to the bottom of the list.

       d.      New CRCI FMS employee will be added to the bottom of the list.

       e.      If there are no volunteers for an overtime assignment, the Institution will
               assign the employee who is at the top of the overtime list to work the
               overtime. If an employee is mandated to work overtime, they will move to
               the bottom of the overtime list.

       f.      If circumstances require a short extension of a shift, up to one (1) hour, to
               complete a task, the Institution may require the employee to complete the
               assignment…Such extension shall not affect the staff’s placement on the
               overtime list. Work beyond one (1) hour will activate the list.

G.     Community Corrections Division: Flexible Schedules
1.     This Subsection shall replace Article 25, Section 1, and Article 15, Section 2,
       regarding the definition of a flexible work schedule and the definition of overtime.

2.     The definition of the flexible work schedule is a work schedule which varies the
       number of hours worked on a daily basis, but which does not exceed forty (40)
       hours in a workweek and is agreed upon in advance by the employee and the
       supervisor.

3.     Overtime shall be defined as employees working a flexible work schedule is time
       in excess of forty (40) hours per workweek.

4.     This Subsection shall only apply if the employee requests a flexible work
       schedule in writing and the Agency approves the flexible work schedule request.

5.     If an employee does not request a flexible work schedule or the Agency does not
       approve the request, then Article 25, Section 1, and Article 15, Section 2, as
       presently written in the Master Agreement shall continue to apply to that
       particular employee.

6.     Nothing in this Subsection changes the authority or ability of the Agency to
       change an employee’s work schedule to meet Agency operating needs in
       accordance with applicable provisions of the Agreement.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               84                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
7.     When a holiday occurs for which the employee would normally be scheduled off,
       the employee’s work schedule will revert back to a five (5) day eight (8) work
       schedule during the calendar week in which the holiday occurs.

8.     This Subsection does not establish any additional obligation on the Employer,
       Agency or the Union to negotiate different workweek or overtime work definitions
       for other bargaining unit employees.

H.     DRCI:
1.     Food Services
       Schedule and Shift Bid:
       a.    The standard workday shall be a period of 24 hours containing 8
             consecutive hours of work interrupted by rest periods.

       b.      Employee assigned as staff relief will be compensated per Article 16,
               Section 10.

       c.      Food service Coordinators who have completed trial service may bid shifts
               in order of seniority. For the purpose of this document seniority shall be
               defined as agency time in class as a Food Service Coordinator. If two (2)
               or more employees have the same time in class, seniority shall then be
               determined by a one (1) time number draw.

       d.      Management will coordinate work assignments and days off and shall
               make the information available to the employees prior to the bid. The bid
               shall take place six (6) weeks prior to the actual changing of shift rotations.
               Schedule change will be posted four (4) weeks prior to starting.

       e.      Shift rotations will occur the first Sunday in the month of February and
               August.

       f.      Food Service Coordinator assignments in the special functions,
               warehouse, and bakery will not be included in the shift rotations. These
               positions are assigned by management through a selection process giving
               deference to specific work experience. These positions are listed on a
               one (1) year rotation when new consideration shall be given by the
               manager. Written criteria will be available at the time of rotations to cover
               the requirements for each position. The potential vacancies shall occur
               within a six (6) month period establishing separate start times. The
               vacancies shall be posted and all interested employees shall submit in
               writing to the manager a request for consideration to fill the post.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                85                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
       g.      Time off requests will be granted by management on a first request basis.
               If two (2) or more employees request the same period of time and the
               matter cannot be resolved the matter will be settled through Article 32,
               Section 10(d).

       h.      Provisions can be made for extraordinary or extenuating circumstances
               that require specific days off. Variances from the days off rotation will be
               allowed after review and mutual agreement by both management and staff
               members.

       Voluntary Overtime:
       If the Institution determines unscheduled overtime work is needed, the Institution
       will offer the overtime opportunity to the employee(s) currently working on shift. If
       there are no volunteers from those employees, the Institution will then call
       employees by seniority and offer the overtime opportunity. The employee who
       volunteers first (1st) will be assigned the overtime.

       Mandatory Overtime:
       If there are no volunteers to work overtime, the overtime will be assigned using
       the mandatory overtime (bucket) list. The list shall be administered in the
       following manner:
               a.    Employee names will be placed on the list in alphabetical order.

               b.      The mandatory overtime list will be published weekly and updated
                       as needed, via email, by Food Service Management or their
                       designee.

               c.      When overtime needs to be assigned, the Institution will assign the
                       employee at the top of the list.

               d.      An employee shall receive credit for working mandatory overtime
                       as follows:
                       1.      Being assigned to work any part of the following shift on
                               mandatory overtime.

                       2.     Being listed as number one (1) on the mandatory overtime
                              list for the week regardless of/if the employee worked
                              overtime on the following shift or not.

                       3.     If an employee calls in on their bucket week their name will
                              remain on the top of the bucket list the following week.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                86                  Collective Bargaining Agreement
               e.      Employee will not be assigned mandatory overtime (bucketed) on
                       their “Friday”.

               f.      No employee shall be required to work more than sixteen (16)
                       hours of mandatory overtime per week, unless there is valid cause.

Employees at the top of the bucket list for PM shift, will cover mandatory overtime for
the following day on AM shift, provided the employee is notified prior to the end of their
shift. An employee shall not be bucketed once they are off site.
2.      Physical Plant
        Overtime:
        a.    When management determines that there is a need for overtime work,
              overtime hours will be offered to FMS employees on a rotating basis
              based on seniority which is defined as Agency time in classification.
              Management will offer overtime work to the most senior employee on the
              list first. If this employee turns down the overtime opportunity,
              management will put that employees name at the bottom of the list and
              the person who is next on the list will be called.

       b.      Ties in seniority will be determined by a double blind lottery system as
               described in Article 44, Section 3d.

       Leadwork:
       A.   Leadwork differential will be paid to employees who are formally assigned,
            in writing, to perform leadwork provided the leadwork duties are not
            included in the classification specification for the employee’s position.
            Leadwork is where an employee has been formally assigned to do
            substantially all of the following for five (5) or more days (or the equivalent
            thereof for alternate or flexible schedules) or longer in a calendar month;
            or five (5) (or the equivalent thereof for alternate or flexible schedules)
            consecutive calendar days or longer that span the end of one (1) month
            and the beginning of the next month:
            1.     To orient new employees, if appropriate.

               2.      Assign and reassign tasks to accomplish prescribed work
                       efficiently.

               3.      Give direction to workers concerning work procedures.

               4.      Transmit established standards of performance to workers.

               5.      Review work of employees for conformance to standards.



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               87                  Collective Bargaining Agreement
               6.      Provide informal assessment of workers’ performance to the
                       supervisor.

       B.      The differential shall be five (5%) percent beginning from the first day the
               duties were formally assigned in writing.

       C.      If an employee receives more than one (1) differential (except overtime as
               mandated by the FLSA), each differential will be calculated on base pay.

       D.      Leadwork differential shall not apply for voluntary training and
               development purposes which are mutually agreed to in writing between
               the supervisor and the employee. Pursuant to Article 21, Section 3, staff
               will not receive both work out of class and leadwork differential.

       E.      If an employee believes that they are performing the duties of a
               leadworker, but the duties have not been formally assigned in writing, they
               may submit the matter for resolution through the grievance procedure (as
               for example, classification review, work out-of-class).

I.     EOCI:
1.     Physical Plant
       The workday will include two (2) fifteen (15)-minute rest periods. Rest periods
       preferably will occur between the second and third hours and the fifth and sixth
       hours of the employee’s shift.

       The meal period shall be scheduled for each employee at approximately the mid
       period of the workday as work permits.

       Powerhouse-Boiler Operators Only.
       a.   Shift Rotation and Work Hours: The Powerhouse staff will rotate shifts
            every six (6) months, becoming effective o the first (1st) Sunday of
            February and August. Employees occupying a position as Boiler Operator
            shall be required to work an eight (8) hours shift without an unpaid meal
            period.

       b.      Overtime: Overtime shall be voluntary except during periods of emergency
               or where management is not able to fill a work assignment by voluntary
               means. Management will offer overtime work to the employee who is at
               the top of the overtime list. If that employee turns down the overtime
               opportunity, management will place that employee’s name at the bottom of
               the list and the employee who is next on the list will be contacted. Once
               an employee works an overtime assignment, their name will be placed at
               the bottom of the list. If no employee volunteers to work the overtime


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               88                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
               assignment, the Institution will require the employee on shift in the boiler
               house to work the mandatory overtime assignment.

       c.      Time Trades: Employees may trade a day or their full shift rotation
               provided all conditions are met under Article 25 Section 6 of the
               Agreement and there is no shift/days off bidding.

       Employees assigned as staff relief will be compensated per Article 16 Section 11
       Staff Relief.

2.     Food Services
       a.    Definition of Workday: The standard workday shall be a period of twenty-
             four (24) hours containing eight (8) consecutive hours of work interrupted
             by rest periods. (Employees shall not receive a paid meal period.)

       b.      Staff Relief Differential: Food Service Coordinators assigned as staff relief
               will be compensated per Article 16, Section 11.

       c.      Rest Breaks: The workday will include two (2) fifteen (15) minute rest
               periods. Rest periods preferably will occur between the second and third
               hours and the fifth and sixth hours of the employee’s shift.

       d.      Filling Vacancies:      Before initiating the normal Agency recruitment
               processes and except for Bakery, Warehouse and Vacation Relief
               positions, when there is a permanent position vacancy that the Institution
               chooses to fill, the Institution will solicit for employee interest. The
               Institution will identify the shift and days off and assigned duties.
               Employees must submit an email or memorandum to the Institution
               expressing interest. The Institution will select an employee for the
               position. If, as a result of an employee being selected for a position,
               another position becomes vacant, the Institution will follow the above
               steps.

       e.      Overtime:
               Where there is scheduled overtime that needs to be worked, the Institution
               will send an email asking for employees to volunteer for the overtime
               work. The first (1st) employee who volunteers to work overtime will be
               assigned.
               1.     If management determines there is a need for overtime to be
                      worked, the Institution will first solicit on-shift volunteers. If there
                      are no on-shift volunteers, management will use the volunteer call-
                      at-home list.



2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                89                    Collective Bargaining Agreement
               2.      If there are no volunteers to work overtime, the overtime will be
                       assigned using the mandatory overtime (bucket) list for on-shift
                       staff. The list shall be administered in the following manner:
                       a.     Employee names will initially be placed on the list in
                              alphabetical order.

                       b.     A mandatory overtime list of all Food Service Coordinators
                              shall be maintained in the food service office and will be
                              accessible to all employees in the Food Service Coordinator
                              folder on the (P) drive.

                       c.     If the manager/assistant manager is unable to find an
                              employee to work a voluntary overtime, the first employee on
                              the mandatory overtime list, who is on duty, will be required
                              to work the overtime.

                       d.     An employee who is not on duty will remain at the top of the
                              list and the next person who is on shift will be required to
                              work the overtime shift.

                       e.     Once an employee has been assigned to work mandatory
                              overtime, management will inform he employee of the
                              anticipated amount of time, and once the employee has
                              worked at least that amount or has been released by
                              management sooner than expected their name will be
                              rotated to the bottom of the list.

                       f.     Unless no other options exist to fill a vacant shift, employees
                              will not be assigned mandatory overtime (bucketed) on their
                              ‘True Friday’.

                       g.     No employee shall be required to work more than sixteen
                              (16) hours of mandatory overtime per week unless there is a
                              valid cause.

       f.      Vacation Leave Requests: If an employee desires to take vacation leave
               the employee must submit a written request to the Institution. The
               Institution shall review the request and approve or deny the request based
               on operating needs. If two (2) or more employees request the same time
               period and the matter can not be resolved the matter will be resolved
               through Article 32, Section 10(d) of the Agreement.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                90                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
3.     Voluntary Overtime Process/Correctional Rehabilitation/Weekend and Holiday
       Front Desk Coverage.
       Whenever there is a scheduled or unscheduled overtime opportunity available,
       the following processes shall be followed:
       a.      Scheduled Overtime Assignments.
               1.    If overtime work is necessary, the Correctional Rehabilitation
                     Manager or their designee will send an email to Correctional
                     Rehabilitation Office Specialists seeking volunteers to work
                     voluntary overtime. The email will ask that those interested
                     respond in a specified period of time.

               2.      Should more than one (1) Correctional Rehabilitation Office
                       Specialist respond expressing an interest to work the overtime, the
                       overtime shall be assigned to the most senior staff. Seniority shall
                       be defined as time in classification inside the Union’s bargaining
                       unit.

               3.      If there are no volunteers to work the overtime from Office
                       Specialists in Correctional Rehabilitation, then the Correctional
                       Rehabilitation Manager or designee shall send out another email to
                       all other Security Plus Office Specialists in the Institution seeking
                       volunteers to work the overtime. Should more than one (1) Office
                       Specialist volunteer to work the overtime, the overtime shall be
                       assigned to the most senior Office Specialist. Seniority is defined
                       as time in classification inside the Union’s bargaining unit. Office
                       Specialists employed outside of Correctional Rehabilitation must
                       obtain training before working overtime at the front desk.

       b.      Unscheduled Overtime Assignments.
               1.   A voluntary overtime phone list shall be established by seniority.
                    Office Specialist from Correctional rehabilitation shall be placed first
                    (1st) on the list with their contact numbers as provided by him/her.
                    Seniority shall be defined as time in classification within the Union’s
                    bargaining unit.

               2.      Office Specialists outside of Correctional Rehabilitation desiring to
                       be placed on this list shall be placed on the list below Correctional
                       Rehabilitation Office Specialists.

               3.      When there is an unscheduled overtime opportunity available, the
                       OIC shall use the list to find a volunteer starting from the top of the
                       list and continuing down the list until a volunteer is identified. A
                       message on voice mail shall be deemed as notification.


2023-2025 DOC Security Plus                 91                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
J.     OISC: Transitional Services Division.
       1.    The Parties agree to a flexible work schedule for employees at the
             discretion of the management. A flexible work schedule is defined as a
             schedule which may vary the number of hours worked on a daily basis
             and the sequencing of these hours, but does not exceed forty (40) hours
             in a workweek as defined in Article 25 Section 1 of the Agreement.

       2.      Overtime shall be defined as time worked in excess of forty (40) hours in a
               workweek. The Parties also expressly agree that this specific provision
               shall supersede the overtime pay provisions contained in Article 15,
               Section 2 Sentence 1 of the Agreement.

       3.      A flexible work schedule applies only if the Agency approves the
               employee’s flexible work schedule request. Requests must be in writing to
               the employee’s immediate supervisory on the Agency’s request form and
               must be approved in advance by the employee’s immediate supervisor.

       4.      When a holiday occurs for which the employee would normally be
               scheduled off, the employee’s work schedule will either revert to a five (5)
               day eight (8) hour work schedule during the workweek in which the holiday
               occurs, or, with management approval, the employee may utilize accrued
               vacation leave or adjust their work schedule in order to assure that the
               regularly scheduled work hours for the workweek in which the holiday
               occurs total no more than thirty-two (32) hours within a forty (40) hour
               workweek.

       5.      Nothing in this agreement changes the authority or the ability of the
               Agency to change an employee’s work schedule to meet Agency
               operating needs in accordance with applicable provisions of the
               Agreement.

       6.      This agreement does not establish any additional obligation on the
               Employer, Agency or Union to negotiate different workweek or overtime
               work definitions for other bargaining unit employees.

K.    OSCI: Food Service Coordinators
The Parties agree to the following for the Food Service Coordinators at Oregon State
Correctional Institution (OSCI):

For the purpose of this section, seniority will be determined by length of time in class
within the Agency.




2023-2025 DOC Security Plus               92                   Collective Bargaining Agreement
Bidding will occur every six (6) months effective on the first Sunday in July and
December. The Food Service Coordinators will bid their scheduled days off by seniority
starting with the most senior employee in descending order.
Available shift and days off will be posted two (2) weeks prior to bid date.

All positions will be included in the bid process and employees shall bid for shift and
days off.

L.    OSPM Food Service*
The Food service staff at OSPM will have the opportunity to bid for four shifts as follows:
      A.M. Shift: (3:30 A.M. to 1:30 P.M.) with Sunday, Monday and Tuesday off.
      Except when Food Services staff are on vacation, training, or has other
      scheduled time off, then five 8 hour days, with Sunday and Monday off.

       P.M. Shift: (8:30 A.M. to 6:30 P.M.) with Thursday, Friday and Saturday off.
       Except when someone is on vacation, training, or has other scheduled time off,
       then five 8 hour days, with Friday and Saturday off.

       Relief Shift: Regular schedule is Sunday, Monday and Tuesday 3:30 A.M. to 1:30
       P.M., Wednesday, Thursday and Friday off and Saturday 7:00 A.M. to 5:00 P.M.
       (varied as needed) 10 hour days except when someone is on vacation, training,
       or has other scheduled time off.

       Relief Shift: (varied shifts) When no one is on vacation Sunday 5:00 A.M. to 3:00
       P.M., Monday, Tuesday and Wednesday off, Thursday, Friday and Saturday 8:30
       A.M. to 6:30 P.M. When relieving for vacation, training, or other scheduled leave
       – eight (8) or ten (10) hour days with varied start times and varied days off
       (Wednesday and Thursday off when working five (5) eight (8) hour days). This
       shift receives 5% differential.

The actual bidding will take place on September 1st (each year) from 1:00 P.M. to 3:00
P.M. with each person getting thirty (30) minutes to bid starting with the most senior
person. Each person is allowed to place three (3) bids for shift and days off. Shifts will
be awarded based on Institution Seniority. The effective start date of the awarded shift
will be the first Sunday of October (each year). It is intended that this schedule will not
cause the ODOC extra dollars and will be budget neutral.
The ODOC reserves the right to change this schedule at any time with seven days
notice to staff.

*NOTE: Language is not operational while OSPM is deactivated.
